## CcoBattleAbility -- Component context wrapper for a battle ability (EMPIREBATTLE::ABILITY_DETAILS) which has live information for an ability like cooldown time, etc. exposes all information available to UiEd
CcoBattleAbility.IntensityCanDecay | Bool | Void | Returns true if ability's intensity can decay |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.IsRechargeEnabled | Bool | Void | Returns true if all recharge contexts for this ability are true (eg engaged in melee) |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.IsPhaseActive | Bool | CcoSpecialAbilityPhaseRecord | Returns true if supplied CcoSpecialAbilityPhaseRecord is active in any of abilities phase trees |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.IsReignOfChaosReward | Bool | Void | Returns true if this is an amry ability that can be rewarded as a reign of chaos outcome |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.ActivePhaseList | CcoPair (list) | Void | Returns list of CcoPair; the active phases for each target essentially |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.TimeUntilIntensityDecay | Int | Void | Returns amount of time (in seconds) until intensity decay starts |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.IsTargettedAbility | Bool | Void | Returns true if this is an ability that you have to select a target for |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.MaxIntensity | Float | Void | Returns the maximum intensity that modifiable stats of this ability will be multiplied by |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.IsActive | Bool | Void | Returns true if this abiltiy is currently active and applying its effects |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.HasLimitedUses | Bool | Void | Returns true if the ability has a limited number of uses (queried by RemainingUses) |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.DefaultIntensity | Float | Void | Returns the starting/default intensity that modifiable stats of this ability will be multiplied by |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.EffectRange | Float | Void | Returns the range in metres that this ability will affect from the cast point |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.Intensity | Float | Void | Returns the intensity that modifiable stats of this ability will be multiplied by |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.IsCurrentlyTargetting | Bool | Void | Returns true if this ability is currently being targetted by the player |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.CurrentState | String | Void | Returns a String representing the current state (active/passive/selected/inactive |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.CanBePurchased | Bool | Void | Returns true if this ability can currently be afforded based on any required currencies (see CurrencyCosts) |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.RemainingUses | Int | Void | Returns the number of remaining uses, -1 if unlimited |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.CurrencyCosts | CcoPair (list) | Void | Returns a list of CcoPair representing the costs required to activate this ability |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.WindUpPercent | Float | Void | Returns the wind up percentage (0-1), 0 being not in windup and 1 being at the point where casts the spell. Wind up is the casting time essentially. |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.RechargeDisabledReasonsText | UniString | Void | Returns joined localised string of disabled reasons for this ability, if any (in melee, etc) |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.WindUpTimeRemainingSecs | Float | Void | Returns the wind up time remaining in seconds. Wind up is the casting time essentially. |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.LeftClickText | UniString | Void | Returns some localised text that explains what left clicking on a button for this ability would currently do (activate, toggle targetting, toggle overcharge, etc) |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.RechargePercent | Float | Void | Returns the cooldown percentage if in cooldown (0-1), 0 if not in cooldown |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.ManaUsed | Int | Void | Returns how much mana is used when activating this ability |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.RechargeTimeRemainingSecs | Float | Void | Returns the cooldown time remaining seconds if ability is in cooldown |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.MiscastChance | Float | Void | Returns 0-1 value representing how likely to miscast when activate. A miscast will have negative impacts on the caster and the ability will fail to activate. |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.CanCurrentlyPerform | Bool | Void | Returns true if abiltiy can currently be activated |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.ActivePercent | Float | Void | Returns the percent (0-1) the ability is through its active duration (progression until ability finishes), 0 if ability is not currently active |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.FillPercent | Float | Void | This is used for circular fills on 3k, where when ability active we unfill the ring (anti-clockwise), then when in cooldown we fill the ring (clockwise), and show the ring full while ability available (so transitions between cooldown being over and ring staying there) |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.IntensityDecayTimerStarted | Bool | Void | Returns true if the timer for the intensity decay has started |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.ActiveTimeRemainingSecs | Float | Void |  |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.IntensityDecayIsActive | Bool | Void | Returns true if intensity decay is active for the ability |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.SetupAbilityContext | CcoUnitAbility | Void | Returns the CcoUnitAbility setup for this ability (fixed information that doesnt change during the battle) |  | 
CcoBattleAbility.PerformAbilityForOwner | Void | Void | Activates the ability for the owner (unit/army) |  | 
## CcoBattleAlliance -- Component context wrapper for a battle alliance, exposes all information available to UiEd
CcoBattleAlliance.IsPlayersAlliance | Bool | Void | Returns true if this is the player controlled alliance |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.IsDefender | Bool | Void | Returns true if this faction is the defender in the battle |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.WillWinOnTimeout | Bool | Void | Returns true if this alliance will win if timer runs out (usually the defender) |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.NumEntitiesInitial | Int | Void | Returns the number of entitie this alliance started the battle with |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.NumEntities | Int | Void | Returns the number of entities in this alliance |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.NumUnits | Int | Void | Returns the number of units in this alliance |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.NumArmies | Int | Void | Returns the number of armies in this alliance |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.CpKillScore | Int | Void | Returns the score for this alliance, the higher the better the alliance performed (earned by unit experience and capturing capture points) |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.StartingTickets | Int | Void | Returns the capture point tickets started the battle with |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.TicketsIncomePerSecond | Int | Void | Returns the total capture point tickets income rate, per second. |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.TicketsRemaining | Int | Void | Returns the capture point tickets remaining (when 0 lose if defending) |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.Id | Int | Void | Returns the index of this alliance |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.ArmyList | CcoBattleArmy (list) | Void | Returns the list of armies in this alliance |  | 
CcoBattleAlliance.DevWinBattle | Void | Void | For dev purposes, forces win for this alliance by killing all units on other alliances in battle | hide_in_uied, debug | 
CcoBattleAlliance.DoesKeepScore | Bool | Void | Returns true if we are keeping scores (CpKillScore) |  |

Can optionally pass the targetting type(OFFENSIVE, DEFENSIVE, OCCUPY) (used to show on tooltip so know how much need to use set target button for example). Returns 0 if no valid targets |  |

## CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry -- Wrapper for a payload entry, a payload is basically a reward from say a mission, this is one entry from the list accessed by CcoCampaignPayloadInfo
CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry.State | String | Void | Returns the state to use in layout, this is either thte rarity state of the ancillary, if no ancillary just returns 'effect' |  |

CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry.Title | UniString | Void | Returns localised name of payload entry reward |  | 
CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry.NumericValue | Int | Void | Returns the numeric value of a treasury reward, pooled resource transaction etc. without the rest of the text description, if not applicable, just returns 0 |  | 
CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry.SortOrder | Int | Void | Returns sort order of the payload |  | 
CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry.UnitRank | Int | Void | Returns the unit rank, will return 0 if the payload doesn't provide a unit |  | 
CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry.IsAccessible | Bool | Void | Returns true if payload is accessible (with DLC permissions) |  | 
CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry.IsUnitPayload | Bool | Void | Returns true if there is a UnitRecordContext |  | 
CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry.UnitRecordContext | CcoMainUnitRecord | Void | Returns the CcoMainUnitRecord provided by the payload if there is any |  | 
CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry.TraitLevelRecordContext | CcoCharacterTraitLevelRecord | Void | Returns the CcoCharacterTraitLevelRecord provided by the payload if there is any |  |

CcoCampaignPayloadInfoEntry.TurnsRemaining | Int | Void | Returns how many turns the payload lasts |  | 
## CcoCampaignPendingAction -- Cco wrapper for getting information on the current pending action in campaign. There can only ever be one hence this being a singleton that basically wraps the pending action manager instead of the pending actions themselves, as would be quite involved to wrap those lifetimes
CcoCampaignPendingAction.Description | UniString | Void | Returns localised description of current pending action |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingAction.ActionType | String | Void | Returns UNlocalised name of current pending action |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingAction.InvolvedFactionList | CcoCampaignFaction (list) | Void | Returns list of factions involved in this action |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingAction.OwningFactionContext | CcoCampaignFaction | Void | Returns owning faction who has main responsibility of resolving this action |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingAction.ZoomTo | Void | Void | Zooms to location if HasZoomLocation is true |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingAction.HasZoomLocation | Bool | Void | Returns true if has a location can zoom to |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingAction.HasTimer | Bool | Void | Returns true if the pending action has a timer |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingAction.TimeRemaining | Float | Void | Returns timer value of the pending action as a float |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingAction.ShouldBlockAllInteractions | Bool | Void | Returns true if IsBlockingLocalPlayer and certain action types (like fullscreen media) which require to disable all interaction with campaign to avoid desyncs |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingAction.IsBlockingLocalPlayer | Bool | Void | Returns true if the current pending action is blocking the local player (as in there is an action and we arent involved) |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingAction.IsActive | Bool | Void | Returns true if there is currently a pending action active (which blocks other players in simultaneous turns mpc) |  |

## CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue -- Component context wrapper for end turn notification system (showing notifications/warnings to take care of before end turn)

CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.PendingActionsOfTypeList | ContextList | String | Returns list of CcoCampaignPendingActionNotification matching specified type string (matching key in notifications table in db) |  |

CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.PendingActionsOfCategoryList | ContextList | String | Returns list of CcoCampaignPendingActionNotification matching specified category (army/character/region/faction) |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.PendingActionList | ContextList | Void | Returns full list of current CcoCampaignPendingActionNotification to be aware of before ending turn |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.SkipCurrentContext | Void | Void | Skips current notification, essentially suppressing it for this turn |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.CycleNotification | Void | Bool | Cycles through the current CurrentContext through NotificationsList (pass true for forwards, false for backwards) |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.SetAllNotificationsSuppressed | Void | Bool | Sets the suppression of all types of notification. |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.ToggleSupressNotificationType | Void | String, Bool (optional) | Toggles suppression of a specific type of notification for specified notification key from db. If pass true as a second parameter, will ignore if db specifies if this can be suppressed (used for script) |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.SetActive | Void | Void | Turns the notification system on |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.ToggleNotifications | Void | Void | Toggles the notification system to be on/off |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.IsNotificationTypeSupressed | Bool | String | Returns true if specified type has been suppressed |  | 
CcoCampaignPendingActionNotificationQueue.IsNotificationListEmpty | Bool | Void | Returns true if there are currently no notifications so can just end turn |  |

## CcoScriptRequirement -- Temporary wrapper for a script-driven unlock requirement shown in UI (e.g. initiative or ritual locks).Script pushes data via common.set_context_value(script_object_id, { [record_key] = { requirement, ... } }). UI reads it with CcoCampaignInitiative.ScriptRequirements(script_object_id) or CcoCampaignRitual.ScriptRequirements(script_object_id).Each requirement table field:- condition_met (bool, optional, default false): whether the requirement is currently met- localisation_key (string, optional): stringtable key for Description- localisation_params (list, optional): format params for the localisation string (max 3 used)- zoom_to (string, optional): ZoomToLink target; leave empty if no zoomEach localisation_params entry is { type, value } where type is:- int: value formatted as decimal- string: value as UniString- localisation_key: value looked up in the stringtablezoom_to supported prefixes: character_cqi:, region_cqi:, building_level_record_key:.
CcoScriptRequirement.ZoomTo | Void | Void | Zooms via ZoomToLink(zoom_to, try_select=true). Supported prefixes: character_cqi:, region_cqi:, building_level_record_key:. Errors if zoom_to is empty. |  | 
CcoScriptRequirement.HasZoomTo | Bool | Void | Returns true if zoom_to is non-empty. Use to show/hide the zoom button. |  | 
CcoScriptRequirement.ConditionMet | Bool | Void | Returns true if condition_met was set true in the script requirement table. |  | 
CcoScriptRequirement.Description | UniString | Void | Returns the localised description from localisation_key, formatted with up to 3 localisation_params. Returns empty if localisation_key is missing. |  |

## CcoScriptObject -- Script adds these via script function effect.set_context_value.Then allows us to leverage context framework so for example could show fill bar for faction awesomeness without requiring any C++ code.Also these can be used in layouts for a global value registry system where can get/set arbitrary global values using ContextInitScriptObject in conjunction with other callbacks like ContextVisibilitySetter and ContextCommandLeftClick for example
CcoScriptObject.Id | String | Void | Returns the unique key of this script object |  | 
CcoScriptObject.SetNumericValue | Void | ExpressionState, Float | Sets the numeric value of the script object from the context system (instead of via script) |  | 
CcoScriptObject.SetStringValue | Void | ExpressionState, String | Sets the string value of the script object from the context system (instead of via script) |  | 
CcoScriptObject.LastComponentThatSetOurValue | String | Void | Returns the id of the last component that set our value | debug, hide_in_uied | 
CcoScriptObject.TableValue | CcoScriptTableNode | Void | If script object for a table, returns root of table which is a recursive type which can point have child table nodes |  | 
CcoScriptObject.StringValue | String | Void | Returns the string value of this script value |  | 
CcoScriptObject.NumericValue | Float | Void | Returns the numeric value of this script value |  | 
## CcoScriptObjectList -- Used purely for Context viewer, has function to expose list of all script objects currently in cache so can track in context viewer
CcoScriptObjectList.ScriptObjectList | ContextList | Void | Returns the list of all script objects currently in cache so can track in the context viewer |  | 
## CcoScriptTableNode -- A wrapper for of a Lua Table, recursive type
CcoScriptTableNode.HasValueForKey | Bool | String, Bool | Returns if there's a value for the key in the table value of the current node, can recurse through children. |  | 
CcoScriptTableNode.ValueForKey | ArgumentPack | String | Returns the value for the matching key in the table value of the current node if present, doesn't traverse more nodes. |  | 
CcoScriptTableNode.FindValueForKey | ArgumentPack | String | Returns the value for the matching key in the table value of the current node if present, will traverse children using DFS. |  | 
CcoScriptTableNode.Value | ArgumentPack | Void | Returns the value of this node, can be a bool, String, numerical value, or another list of nodes |  | 
CcoScriptTableNode.Key | ArgumentPack | Void | Returns the key of this node, can be a bool, String, numerical value, or another list of nodes |  |
